```
def fft_conv_and_corr(a, w, size):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:2*len(a)-1]
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], size)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, size)
    g = h[len(a)-1:2*len(a)-1][::-1]
    return c, g
```

```
def self_conv_and_corr(a, w, size):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    n = len(a)
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    c = np.where(c > 0, c, 0.0)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], size)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, size)
    g = h[n-1:2*n-1]
    g = g[::-1].copy()
    g = np.where(g > 0, g, 0.0)
    return c, g
```

Parametric seeds functions:
```
def make_power_seed(n, p, q, rng=None):
    if n<=1: return np.ones(1)
    r = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    eps=1e-8
    a = (r+eps)**p * (1-r+eps)**q
    # maybe if p,q negative underflow; use np.power with max
    a = np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: a=np.ones(n)
    else: a=a/s*n
    return a
```
Power with p negative: r=0, eps ok. Use eps=1e-6 maybe.

Fourier seed:
```
def make_fourier_seed(n, D=3, max_amp=0.8, rng=np.random.default_rng(0)):
   r=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   a=np.ones(n)
   amp_total=max_amp
   for m in range(1,D+1):
       coef = amp_total * (0.5 + rng.random())? Ensure sum coef<1.
   # simpler: coefficients = rng.uniform(0,0.3,D), total=sum coeff; normalize to max_amp
   for j,m in enumerate(1..D):
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       a += coeff[j]*np.cos(2π*m*r + phase) # use cos/sin via phase
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-8); normalize
```

```
def optimize_y_optimizer(y0, n, deadline, best_score, best_a, gamma_start, gamma_min, gamma_max, lr_start, exact_interval, max_exact, random_seed, label=""):
```
Return `(best_score, best_a, y_end)`.
But cannot update best_a if passed reference? Use local and return.

Structure:
```
rng=np.random.default_rng(random_seed)
y=y0.copy()
a=vald? y_to_a
m1=np.zeros(n); m2=np.zeros(n)
v? maybe Adam.
local_best_fast=np.inf; local_best_a=None
exact_count=0; last_exact=time.monotonic()
gamma=gamma_start; lr=lr_start
stall=0; no_improve=0; exact_limit=max(1,max_exact)
print start
while time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
   # update y? Need each iter
   a=y_to_a(y,n)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
   cfull=irfft(fa*fa,size)[:2n-1]; c=np.maximum(cfull,0)
   M=c.max()
   if not finite: break
   fast=2*M/n  # since sum n
   if fast < local_best_fast - 1e-12:
       local_best_fast=fast; local_best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
       if best_a is None: ...
   else:
       no_improve +=1
   # exact eval conditions
   if local_best_a is not None and (fast < best_score - 1e-7 or exact_count<1 or no_improve==0) and time since last_exact > exact_interval and exact_count<exact_limit and remaining >60:
       sc=safe_exact(local_best_a)
       if finite and sc<best_score:
           best_score=sc; best_a=scale_down(local_best_a) # maybe keep scaled? best_a for comparison can be scaled; but optimizer uses y. For return, scaled.
       exact_count +=1; last_exact=time...
       # If improved, maybe print
   # weights
   w=compute_weights(c,M,gamma)
   g=grad_from_weights(a,w,fa,size)
   # gradient maybe normalize: if max-min too small, reduce?
   gm=g-g.mean()
   # Adam
   b1=0.9; b2=0.999
   m1 = b1*m1 + (1-b1)*gm
   m2 = b2*m2 + (1-b1)*gm*gm  # correct b1? use (1-b2)
   denom=np.sqrt(m2)+1e-8
   y = y - lr * m1/denom
   # scale bias? Adam initial step maybe large due m1 small? Could do bias correction:
   step=lr * (m1/(np.sqrt(m2)+1e-8)) * (t*?) maybe. Use no bias, first grad small.
   # y drift/range
   if not finite y: reset y=y0 or local; m1=m2=0; lr=lr_start
   yrange=y.max()-y.min()
   if yrange>120: y=(y-y.mean())*(120/yrange)
   y -= y.mean() # optional center
   # adaptive
   if no_improve >= 30:
       lr *= 0.75
       gamma = max(gamma_min, gamma*0.8)
       # clear momentum
       m1*=0.2; m2*=0.2
       no_improve=0
   if no_improve == 0 and gamma < gamma_max:
       gamma = min(gamma_max, gamma*1.01) # increase slowly on improvement
   # restart
   if no_improve >= 150 or lr < 1e-5:
       if local_best_a is not None:
           y=make_y_from_a(local_best_a)
       else: y=y0.copy()
       lr=lr_start*0.5
       gamma=gamma_start
       m1=np.zeros(n); m2=np.zeros(n)
       no_improve=0
       # perturb
       y += rng.normal(0, 0.03, n)
```
But `no_improve` increments every time fast not below local best. Since local best updates only on strict improvement, after a while no_improve huge. That triggers restart often too early. Use `stall` count but with tolerance: improvement threshold `tol=1e-7` maybe. `if fast < local_best_fast - 1e-9:`. But fast may oscillate. Use `if fast < local_best_fast - 1e-10`? We also need initial local best set at first iteration; exact maybe.

In loop:
- if fast improves local: improve_time=now.
- if now - improve_time > 20: lr*=0.7, gamma*=0.9; clear some momentum.
- if now - improve_time > 90: restart around local best with noise, reset lr/gamma.
Also if time near deadline, stop.

Now, high-dimensional y optimizer may need good `gamma` and `lr`. Let's maybe use two variants: one y-Adam, one linear top-peak. We can implement both and run as candidates.

Linear optimizer using logsum weights:
```
def optimize_linear(a0, n, deadline, ...):
  a=normalize(a0,n)
  momentum=np.zeros(n)
  lr=0.05
  gamma=20
  m1/m2 maybe for gradient? Use simple heavy ball.
  loop:
   fa, c, M, fast
   update best
   w=weights(gamma)
   g=grad
   grad=g-g.mean()
   momentum=0.8*momentum - lr*grad
   a=np.maximum(a+momentum,0)
   a=normalize_to_sum(a,n)
   adapt lr/stall/restart
```

```
def resize_sequence(a, n):
   if len(a)==n: return a.copy()
   old=np.asarray(a); i_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(old)); i_new=np.linspace(0,1,n); return np.interp(i_new,i_old,old)
```

`def optimize_y(y0, n, deadline, best_score, best_a, run_label, exact_interval, max_exact, gamma0, lr0)`:
- `size = pow2_at_least(3*n-2)` maybe for n=1 size=1.
- y = make_y... If y invalid, uniform.
- a = y_to_a(y,n)
- m1 = zeros, m2 = zeros
- local_fast = inf, local_a = None
- start_time=now
- gamma=gamma0, lr=lr0
- last_improve=now
- last_exact=now, exact_count=0

Loop:
```
while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
   t = time.monotonic()
   a = y_to_a(y,n)
   fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
   cfull = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:2*n-1]
   c = np.maximum(cfull,0)
   M = c.max()
   if not np.isfinite(M) or M <=0: break
   fast = 2.0*M/n
   if fast < local_fast - 1e-12:
       local_fast=fast; local_a=a.copy(); last_improve=t
       # maybe exact if fast < best_score - 1e-7 and t-last_exact>exact_interval
   if local_a is not None and exact_count<max_exact and fast < best_score - 1e-7 and t-last_exact>exact_interval and deadline-t>60:
       sc = safe_evaluate(scale_down(local_a))
       if finite and sc < best_score:
           best_score=sc; best_a=scale_down(local_a).copy()
       exact_count+=1; last_exact=t
       if exact_count==max_exact: last_exact=inf
   # weights and gradient
   w = make_weights(c, M, gamma)
   g = grad_from_weights(a,w,fa,size)
   grad = g - g.mean()
   # if grad range tiny (uniform peak), add tiny random? not every iter.
   m1 = 0.9*m1 + 0.1*grad
   m2 = 0.999*m2 + 0.001*grad*grad
   step = lr * m1 / (np.sqrt(m2)+1e-8)
   y = y - step
   # if y nonfinite, reset
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
       if local_a is not None: y=make_y_from_a(local_a)
       else: y=np.zeros(n)
       m1=m2=0; lr=lr0; gamma=gamma0; last_improve=t
   y -= y.mean()
   yr = y.max()-y.min()
   if yr > 100: y = y * (100/yr)
   # adapt gamma: gradually increase but if stale reduce
   elapsed = t-start_time
   if elapsed < 30: gamma_target = gamma0 + elapsed*0.3
   elif elapsed < 120: gamma_target = gamma0+9 + (elapsed-30)*0.8
   else: gamma_target = min(gamma_max, gamma0+9+72 + (elapsed-120)*0.2)
   # smooth gamma toward target if improving, else decay
   if t-last_improve < 30: gamma = min(gamma_max, gamma + max(0.0, gamma_target - gamma)*0.1)
   else: gamma = max(gamma_min, gamma*0.98)
   # adapt lr and restart based on stale
   stale = t-last_improve
   if stale > 20:
       lr *= 0.7
       m1 *= 0.3; m2 *= 0.3
       # maybe if still improving slowly? no
   if stale > 80:
       # restart around local best with noise
       if local_a is not None:
           y = make_y_from_a(local_a)
       else:
           y = y0.copy()
       y = y + np.random.default_rng((int(elapsed)+1617)).normal(0, 0.05, n) # but creating rng each restart okay
       m1=np.zeros(n); m2=np.zeros(n)
       lr=lr0*0.6
       gamma=gamma0
       last_improve=t
```

Implementation details:
```
size arrays; results=[]
if include_parent and parent is not None:
   a=_resize(parent,n); normalize; results.append(a)
# power grid
r=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
powers = [-0.75,-0.5,-0.25,0,0.25,0.5,1,1.5]
for p in powers:
  for q in powers:
    if p==0 and q==0: continue? uniform add once.
    a=(r+1e-6)**p * (1-r+1e-6)**q
    # if p,q large, overflow? use clip
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0, posinf=0, neginf=0); max; normalize; add
# tilts
for beta in [-3,-2,-1,-0.5,0.5,1,2,3]: a=np.exp(beta*r)
# sine fouriers
modes_pool = np.array([1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8,10,12,16,20])
for trial in range(60):
  D = int(rng.integers(1,5)) # 1..4
  modes = rng.choice(modes_pool, size=D, replace=False)
  weights = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(D))
  amp = float(rng.uniform(0.4, 1.0))
  sign = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
  pat = np.zeros(n)
  for m,w in zip(modes,weights): pat += w*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*r)
  a = 1.0 + sign*amp*pat
  a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
  add
# endpoint*fourier
for trial in range(30):
```

```
def _normalize_valid(a,n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size != n: ...? We can pass.
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = a.sum()
    if S < 1e-12:
       return None
    return a / S * n
```

```
def _generate_seeds(n, parent, rng, max_scored=120):
    seeds=[]
```

```
def optimize_y(y0, n, deadline, best_score, best_a, label, exact_interval, max_exact, gamma0, lr0, rng_seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)
    size = _pow2(3*n - 2)
    y = np.asarray(y0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if y.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        y = np.zeros(n)
    y = _make_y_from_y? Actually y0 already log normalized. But ensure finite, mean 0.
    y -= y.mean()
    yr = y.max()-y.min(); if yr>80: y=y*(80/yr)
    a = _y_to_a(y,n)
    local_fast=np.inf; local_a=None
    gamma=gamma0; lr=lr0
    last_improve=time.monotonic(); last_exact=time.monotonic(); exact_count=0
    m1=np.zeros(n); m2=np.zeros(n)
    start=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    print(f"[{label}] start n={n} gamma={gamma0}")
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
       it+=1
       a=_y_to_a(y,n)
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
       cfull=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)[:2*n-1]
       c=np.maximum(cfull,0.0)
       M=float(c.max())
       if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0: break
       fast=2.0*M/n
       tnow=time.monotonic()
       if fast < local_fast - 1e-12:
           local_fast=fast; local_a=a.copy(); last_improve=tnow
       # exact
       if local_a is not None and exact_count<max_exact and fast < best_score - 1e-7 and tnow-last_exact>exact_interval and deadline-tnow>30:
           sc=_safe_evaluate(_scale_for_eval(local_a))
           exact_count +=1; last_exact=tnow
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
               best_score=sc; best_a=_scale_for_eval(local_a).copy()
               print(f"[{label}] exact improved {sc:.8f} it={it}")
       # weights
       w = _make_weights(c,M,gamma)
       g = _grad_from_weights(a,w,fa,size)
       grad = g - g.mean()
       # If grad all tiny, maybe add tiny random to escape? not.
       m1 = 0.9*m1 + 0.1*grad
       m2 = 0.999*m2 + 0.001*grad*grad
       step = lr * m1 / (np.sqrt(m2) + 1e-6)
       max_step = np.max(np.abs(step))
       if max_step > 0.8:
           step = step * (0.8/max_step)
       y = y - step
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
           if local_a is not None:
               y = _make_y_from_a(local_a)
           else:
               y = np.zeros(n)
           m1.fill(0); m2.fill(0)
           lr=lr0; gamma=gamma0
       y -= y.mean()
       yr = y.max()-y.min()
       if yr > 100.0:
           y = y * (100.0/yr)
       # gamma schedule
       elapsed=tnow-start
       if elapsed < 30:
           target = gamma0 + elapsed*0.2
       elif elapsed < 120:
           target = gamma0 + 6 + (elapsed-30)*0.8
       else:
           target = min(300.0, gamma0 + 78 + (elapsed-120)*0.15)
       stale = tnow - last_improve
       if stale < 10:
           target = max(gamma, gamma*1.001)
       gamma = min(300.0, max(1.0, 0.9*gamma + 0.1*target))
       if stale > 20:
           lr *= 0.7
           m1 *= 0.3; m2 *= 0.3
       if stale > 90:
           if local_a is not None:
               y = _make_y_from_a(local_a)
           else:
               y = np.zeros(n)
           y += rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n)
           m1.fill(0); m2.fill(0)
           lr = lr0*0.7
           gamma = gamma0
           last_improve = tnow
    # after loop exact local best if finite and not too large
    if local_a is not None and exact_count < max_exact+1 and np.isfinite(local_fast):
        sc = _safe_evaluate(_scale_for_eval(local_a))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score=sc; best_a=_scale_for_eval(local_a).copy()
    else if local_a and best_score maybe inf? ...
    print(f"[{label}] done fast={local_fast:.8f}")
    return best_score, best_a
```

```
def _distribute_budget(budget, num):
    if num<=0: return []
    fracs = []
    if num==1: [1]
    elif num==2: [0.55,0.45]
    elif num==3: [0.45,0.3,0.25]
    else: [0.35,0.25,0.2,0.2,...]
    return [budget*f for f in fracs]
```

Top functions:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _pow2_at_least(m):
    p=1
    if m < 1: m=1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p
```

`_validate_sequence`:
```
def _validate_sequence(seq, fallback_n=256):
    if seq is None:
        return np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        a=np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    if a.sum() < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(max(fallback_n,a.size) if a.size<2? )? 
    return a
```
If seq list bool? np.asarray float converts. OK. If all zeros, uniform length a.size (maybe 2535). Use `np.ones(a.size if a.size>0 else fallback_n)`.

`_normalize_valid(a,n)` as above.

```
def _normalize_valid(a, n):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size != n:
       # resize?
       a=_resize_sequence(a,n)
    a=np.nan_to_num(...)
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12: return None
    return a/S*n
```

`_resize_sequence(a,n)`:
```
def _resize_sequence(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    m=a.size
    if n<=0: return np.array([],dtype=float)
    if m==0: return np.ones(n)
    if m==n: return a.copy()
    if m==1: return np.full(n, float(a[0]))
    old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    return np.interp(new, old, a)
```

`_scale_for_eval(a, cap=1000.0)` as above.

```
def _safe_evaluate(a):
    a=_scale_for_eval(a)
    if a.size==0 or a.sum()<0.01: return np.inf
    try:
       val=float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
    except Exception:
       return np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(val): return np.inf
    return val
```

`_fft_self(a)`:
```
def _fft_self(a):
   n=len(a)
   if n<=1: return np.array([float(a[0])**2])
   size=_pow2_at_least(2*n-1)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   return c
```

`_fast_score(a)`:
```
def _fast_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=len(a); S=a.sum()
   if n==0 or S<0.01: return np.inf
   c=_fft_self(a)
   M=c.max()
   if not finite: return inf
   return float(2*n*M/(S*S))
```
For our optimization a sum n, fast=2M/n. But use general.

`_make_y_from_a(a)`:
```
 def _make_y_from_a(a):
    n=len(a)
    a=_normalize_valid(a,n)
    if a is None: return np.zeros(n)
    maxv=float(a.max())
    eps=max(1e-300, 1e-15*max(1.0,maxv))
    a=np.maximum(a, eps)
    y=np.log(a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)): return np.zeros(n)
    y -= y.mean()
    rng = y.max()-y.min()
    if rng > 80.0 and rng>0:
        y = y * (80.0/rng)
    return y
```

```
   S=a.sum()

   return a/S*n
```

`_make_weights(c, M, gamma)`:
```
def _make_weights(c, M, gamma):
   L=len(c)
   if M <=0 or not finite: return np.full(L,1.0/L)
   ratio=c/M
   ratio=np.clip(ratio,0.0,1.0)
   if gamma <=0: return np.full(L,1.0/L)
   scaled=gamma*(ratio-1.0)
   # maybe if gamma>700 clip to -700
   if gamma > 700: scaled=np.maximum(scaled, -700.0)
   w=np.exp(scaled)
   ws=w.sum()
   if ws<=0 or not finite:
      w=np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
   else: w/=ws
   return w
```
At ratio max 1, scaled 0. If c all zero M=0.

```
def _grad_from_weights(a,w,fa,size):
   n=len(a)
   if n==1:
      return np.array([2.0*w[0]*a[0]])? For derivative factor maybe; But g definition ∑ w a_{k-i}. For n=1, w length1, g0=a0*w0. Use no factor. Return max(...,0)
   fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], size)
   h=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, size)
   g=h[n-1:2*n-1]
   g=g[::-1].copy()
   g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
```

`_generate_seeds` as above. Need pass `scored_limit` and top_k.

```
def _generate_seeds(n, parent, seed_int, top_k=4, max_fourier=250, max_endpoint=60, max_scored=300):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed_int)
    raw=[]
    if parent is not None:
       na=_normalize_valid(_resize_sequence(parent,n), n)
       if na is not None: raw.append(na)
    # uniform
    na=_normalize_valid(np.ones(n,dtype=float),n); if naive not None raw.append(na)
    if n <= 1: ... return raw top
    r = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    eps=1e-8
    pvals=(-0.75,-0.5,-0.25,0.0,0.25,0.5,1.0,1.5)
    for p in pvals:
       for q in pvals:
          if p==0.0 and q==0.0: continue
          a=np.power(r+eps, p)*np.power(1.0-r+eps, q)
          a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
          na=_normalize_valid(a,n); if naive not None: raw.append(na)
    # tilts
    for beta in (-3.0,-2.0,-1.0,-0.5,0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0):
       a=np.exp(beta*r)
       na=_normalize_valid(a,n); raw.append
    modes_pool=np.array([1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8,10,12,16,20], dtype=np.int64)
    for _ in range(max_fourier):
       D=int(rng.integers(1,6))
       modes=rng.choice(modes_pool, size=D, replace=False)
       coeffs=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,D)
       norm=float(np.linalg.norm(coeffs))
       if norm < 1e-12: continue
       coeffs=coeffs/norm
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.0))
       pat=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
       for j in range(D):
           pat += coeffs[j]*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*float(modes[j])*r)
       maxpat=float(np.max(np.abs(pat)))
       if maxpat > 1e-12:
           amp = min(amp, 0.95/maxpat) # preserve nonnegative? if amp*maxpat <=0.95, a min >=0.05.
       a=1.0+amp*pat
       a=np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
       na=_normalize_valid(a,n); if naive not None: raw.append(na)
    for _ in range(max_endpoint):
       p=float(rng.uniform(-0.7,1.2)); q=float(rng.uniform(-0.7,1.2))
       base=np.power(r+eps,p)*np.power(1.0-r+eps,q)
       base=np.nan_to_num(base, nan=0, posinf=0, neginf=0)
       D=int(rng.integers(1,5)); modes = ...; coeffs = rng.normal; norm; amp=uniform(0.2,0.9); maxpat adjust; pat...
       a=base*(1.0 + amp*pat)
       a=np.maximum(a,1e-8)
       na=_normalize_valid(a,n); if naive not None raw.append
    # random logit
    for _ in range(30):
       spec=np.fft.rfft(rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n), n)
       spec[0]=0.0
       freq_idx=np.arange(spec.shape[0])
       mask=np.exp(-2.0*freq_idx/max(1,n//10))
       spec=spec*mask
       y=np.fft.irfft(spec,n)
       y-=y.mean()
       scale=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8))
       a=np.exp(scale*y)
       na=_normalize_valid(a,n); if naive not None raw.append
    # limit raw
    if len(raw)>max_scored:
       # keep first two (parent/uniform) and sample rest
       keep=max(2, top_k)
       rest=np.arange(min(2,len(raw)), len(raw))
       if rest.size <= max_scored-keep:
           sample=rest
       else:
           sample=rng.choice(rest, size=max_scored-keep, replace=False)
       raw=[raw[0], raw[1]] + [raw[int(i)] for i in sample] if len(raw)>=2 else raw
    scored=[]
    for a in raw:
       if a is None: continue
       s=_fast_score(a)
       if np.isfinite(s):
          scored.append((s,a))
    if not scored:
       # fallback uniform
       a=_normalize_valid(np.ones(n),n)
       return [a] if a is not None else []
    scores=np.array([s for s,a in scored], dtype=float)
    order=np.argsort(scores)
    top=[]
    for idx in order:
       a=scored[int(idx)][1]
       top.append(a)
       if len(top)>=top_k: break
```

```
def _allocate_durations(budget, count):
   if count <=0 or budget <=0: return []
   if count >= len? use fracs list
   fracs=[0.5,0.25,0.2,0.15,0.1,0.1,0.1]
   if count==1: fracs=[1.0]
   elif count==2: fracs=[0.55,0.45]
   else: fracs=fracs[:count]
   # normalize sum fracs to 1
   s=sum(fracs); fracs=[f/s for f in fracs]
   return [budget*f for f in fracs]
```

Let's simulate some parts for syntax.

In `_distribute_durations`:
```
def _distribute_durations(budget, count):
    if count <= 0 or budget <= 0.0:
        return []
    if count == 1:
        return [budget]
    fracs = [0.5, 0.25, 0.2, 0.15, 0.1, 0.1, 0.1]
    fracs = fracs[:count]
    s = sum(fracs)
    if s <= 0:
        fracs = [1.0 / count] * count
    else:
        fracs = [f / s for f in fracs]
    return [budget * f for f in fracs]
```
List comprehension okay. No lambda.

In `_generate_seeds`, after raw limit, if raw includes None? It shouldn't; but when scoring skip None.

When keeping raw first two: if len(raw)>0 etc. Use variables:
```
    if len(raw) > max_scored:
        keep = []
        if len(raw) > 0: keep.append(raw[0])
        if len(raw) > 1: keep.append(raw[1])
        start = len(keep)
        want = max_scored - start
        if want < 0: want = 0
        rest = raw[start:]
        if want == 0:
            raw = keep
        elif len(rest) <= want:
            raw = keep + rest
        else:
            rest_idx = np.arange(len(rest))
            sample = rng.choice(rest_idx, size=want, replace=False)
            raw = keep + [rest[int(i)] for i in sample]
```
If raw length 1 and max_scored? OK.

Then allocate.

Parent run deadline: `pdeadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + min(500.0, budget * 0.5))`. But if `budget*0.5` > remaining? budget computed from remaining, so okay. Need ensure pdeadline > now+30 else skip. If parent run takes full parent_dur, remaining_budget for seeds recompute: `remaining_budget = budget - (time.monotonic() - tgen)`? Actually budget originally length budget. After parent run, used = time.monotonic()-tgen. remaining_budget = max(0,budget - used). Then allocate seeds. Good.

In nonparent, after generation consumed, allocate.

Exact intervals in opt: For parent n maybe 2535, interval 3, max_exact 80? But exact calls could be many and consume. Use max_exact 50 for small. In code:
```
if n <= 3500: exact_interval=3.0; max_exact=60
elif n <= 7000: exact_interval=5.0; max_exact=25
else: exact_interval=8.0; max_exact=12
```
Parent 60 exacts maybe okay. In optimize_y, exact condition includes `deadline - tnow > 30`, so near end no. Good.

Now, final linear polish exact max small.

One possible issue: The exact `evaluate_sequence` for n=8192 in optimizer 12 times maybe direct conv 67M*12=804M operations, maybe 1-3s. OK. For n=2535 60*6.4M=384M, maybe 1s. Good.

Now, if `np.convolve` for 8192 is implemented in Python loops? Numpy's multiply loop C, 67M double ops maybe 0.05s; 12=0.6s. Fine.

Now, let's check final exact threshold: `if deadline - time.monotonic() > 25.0:`. If exact takes 10s, safe. For 8192 fine.

Now, any issue with using `globals()` inside lower privileged? Should be fine.

Now, possible improvement: In `_safe_evaluate`, scaled max to 1000. If sequence sum after scaling is huge (n*1000 if uniform) =8e6, conv sum 6e13, double precision has 15 digits; max coefficient maybe 8e9? Relative precision 1e-15, score error tiny. Good.

Now, if sequence has max 1000 and many values, small values underflow? no.

Now, let's proceed to final code. Ensure fenced python only.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def _pow2_at_least(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m < 1:
        m = 1
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _validate_sequence(seq, fallback_n=256):
    if seq is None:
        return np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if float(a.sum()) < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(a.size if a.size > 0 else fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
    return a


def _resize_sequence(a, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    m = a.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(new, old, a)


def _normalize_valid(a, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    a = _resize_sequence(a, n)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return a / s * n


def _scale_for_eval(a, cap=1000.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    mx = float(a.max())
    if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx) or mx < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (cap / mx)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=cap, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, cap)
    return a


def _safe_evaluate(a):
    a = _scale_for_eval(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    if float(a.sum()) < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    try:
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return np.inf
    return val


def _fft_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0]) * float(a[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    size = _pow2_at_least(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    c = _fft_self(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _make_y_from_a(a):
    a = _normalize_valid(a, int(np.asarray(a).size if a is not None and np.asarray(a).size else 1))
    if a is None:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    mx = float(a.max())
    eps = max(1e-300, 1e-15 * max(1.0, mx))
    a2 = np.maximum(a, eps)
    y = np.log(a2)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y -= y.mean()
    span = float(y.max() - y.min())
    if span > 80.0 and span > 0.0:
        y = y * (80.0 / span)
    return y


def _y_to_a(y, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    if y.size != n:
        y = _resize_sequence(y, n)
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    ym = y - float(y.max())
    a = np.exp(ym)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s * n


def _make_weights(c, m, gamma):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    ln = c.size
    if ln == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.full(ln, 1.0 / ln, dtype=np.float64)
    if gamma <= 0.0:
        return np.full(ln, 1.0 / ln, dtype=np.float64)
    ratio = c / m
    ratio = np.clip(ratio, 0.0, 1.0)
    scaled = float(gamma) * (ratio - 1.0)
    if float(gamma) > 700.0:
        scaled = np.maximum(scaled, -700.0)
    w = np.exp(scaled)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        w = np.zeros(ln, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / ws
    return w


def _grad_from_weights(a, w, fa, size):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], size)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, size)
    g = h[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    g = g[::-1].copy()
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    return g


def _allocate_durations(budget, count):
    count = int(count)
    budget = float(budget)
    if count <= 0 or budget <= 0.0:
        return []
    if count == 1:
        return [budget]
    fracs = [0.5, 0.25, 0.2, 0.15, 0.1, 0.1, 0.1]
    fracs = fracs[:count]
    s = sum(fracs)
    if s <= 0.0:
        fracs = [1.0 / float(count)] * count
    else:
        fracs = [x / s for x in fracs]
    return [budget * x for x in fracs]


def _generate_seeds(n, parent, seed_int, top_k=4, max_fourier=220, max_endpoint=70, max_scored=260):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed_int))
    raw = []

    if parent is not None:
        na = _normalize_valid(_resize_sequence(parent, n), n)
        if na is not None:
            raw .append(na)

    na = _normalize_valid(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)
    if na is not None:
        raw.append(na)

    r = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    eps = 1e-4
    pvals = (-0.75, -0.5, -0.25, 0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5)
    for p in pvals:
        for q in pvals:
            if p == 0.0 and q == 0.0:
                continue
            a = np.power(r + eps, p) * np.power(1.0 - r + eps, q)
            a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            na = _normalize_valid(a, n)
            if na is not None:
                raw.append(na)

    for beta in (-3.0, -2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        a = np.exp(beta * r)
        na = _normalize_valid(a, n)
        if na is not None:
            raw.append(na)

    modes_pool = np.array([1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 10, 12, 16, 20], dtype=np.int64)

    for _ in range(max_fourier):
        d = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
        modes = rng.choice(modes_pool, size=d, replace=False)
        coeffs = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, d)
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(coeffs))
        if norm < 1e-12:
            continue
        coeffs = coeffs / norm
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0))
        pat = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(d):
            pat += coeffs[j] * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(modes[j]) * r)
        mp = float(np.max(np.abs(pat)))
        if mp > 1e-12:
            amp = min(amp, 0.95 / mp)
        a = 1.0 + amp * pat
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
        na = _normalize_valid(a, n)
        if na is not None:
            raw.append(na)

    for _ in range(max_endpoint):
        p = float(rng.uniform(-0.7, 1.2))
        q = float(rng.uniform(-0.7, 1.2))
        base = np.power(r + eps, p) * np.power(1.0 - r + eps, q)
        base = np.nan_to_num(base, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        d = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
        modes = rng.choice(modes_pool, size=d, replace=False)
        coeffs = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, d)
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(coeffs))
        if norm < 1e-12:
            continue
        coeffs = coeffs / norm
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.9))
        pat = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(d):
            pat += coeffs[j] * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(modes[j]) * r)
        mp = float(np.max(np.abs(pat)))
        if mp > 1e-12:
            amp = min(amp, 0.90 / mp)
        a = base * (1.0 + amp * pat)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
        na = _normalize_valid(a, n)
        if na is not None:
            raw.append(na)

    for _ in range(35):
        spec = np.fft.rfft(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n), n)
        spec[0] = 0.0
        freq_idx = np.arange(spec.shape[0])
        mask = np.exp(-2.0 * freq_idx / max(1, n // 10))
        spec = spec * mask
        y = np.fft.irfft(spec, n)
        y -= y.mean()
        scale = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        a = np.exp(scale * y)
        na = _normalize_valid(a, n)
        if na is not None:
            raw.append(na)

    if len(raw) > max_scored:
        keep = []
        if len(raw) > 0:
            keep.append(raw[0])
        if len(raw) > 1:
            keep.append(raw[1])
        start = len(keep)
        want = max_scored - start
        if want < 0:
            want = 0
        rest = raw[start:]
        if want == 0:
            raw = keep
        elif len(rest) <= want:
            raw = keep + rest
        else:
            rest_idx = np.arange(len(rest))
            sample = rng.choice(rest_idx, size=want, replace=False)
            raw = keep + [rest[int(i)] for i in sample]

    scored = []
    for a in raw:
        if a is None:
            continue
        sc = _fast_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, a))

    if not scored:
        a = _normalize_valid(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)
        if a is None:
            return []
        return [a]

    scores = np.array([x[0] for x in scored], dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    top = []
    for idx in order:
        top.append(scored[int(idx)][1])
        if len(top) >= top_k:
            break
    return top


def optimize_y(y0, n, deadline, best_score, best_a, label, exact_interval, max_exact, gamma0, lr0, rng_seed):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return best_score, best_a
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(rng_seed))
    size = _pow2_at_least(3 * n - 2)
    y = np.asarray(y0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if y.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    y -= y.mean()
    span = float(y.max() - y.min())
    if span > 100.0 and span > 0.0:
        y = y * (100.0 / span)

    a = _y_to_a(y, n)
    m1 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    local_fast = np.inf
    local_a = None
    gamma = float(gamma0)
    lr = float(lr0)
    start_t = time.monotonic()
    last_improve = start_t
    last_exact = start_t
    exact_count = 0
    it = 0

    print(f"[{label}] start n={n} gamma0={gamma:.2f} lr0={lr:.4f}", flush=True)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0:
        it += 1
        a = _y_to_a(y, n)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
        cfull = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:2 * n - 1]
        c = np.maximum(cfull, 0.0)
        m = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            break
        fast = 2.0 * m / float(n)
        tnow = time.monotonic()

        if fast < local_fast - 1e-12:
            local_fast = fast
            local_a = a.copy()
            last_improve = tnow

        if (
            local_a is not None
            and exact_count < max_exact
            and fast < float(best_score) - 1e-6
            and (tnow - last_exact) > exact_interval
            and (deadline - tnow) > 30.0
        ):
            sc = _safe_evaluate(local_a)
            exact_count += 1
            last_exact = tnow
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = _scale_for_eval(local_a).copy()
                print(f"[{label}] exact improved {sc:.8f} it={it}", flush=True)

        w = _make_weights(c, m, gamma)
        g = _grad_from_weights(a, w, fa, size)
        grad = 2.0 * (g - g.mean())

        m1 = 0.9 * m1 + 0.1 * grad
        m2 = 0.999 * m2 + 0.001 * (grad * grad)
        step = lr * m1 / (np.sqrt(m2) + 1e-6)
        mx_step = float(np.max(np.abs(step))) if step.size else 0.0
        if mx_step > 0.8:
            step = step * (0.8 / mx_step)
        y = y - step

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
            if local_a is not None:
                y = _make_y_from_a(local_a)
            else:
                y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            m1.fill(0.0)
            m2.fill(0.0)
            lr = float(lr0)
            gamma = float(gamma0)
            last_improve = tnow

        y -= y.mean()
        span = float(y.max() - y.min())
        if span > 100.0 and span > 0.0:
            y = y * (100.0 / span)

        elapsed = tnow - start_t
        if elapsed < 30.0:
            target_gamma = gamma0 + elapsed * 0.2
        elif elapsed < 120.0:
            target_gamma = gamma0 + 6.0 + (elapsed - 30.0) * 0.8
        else:
            target_gamma = min(300.0, gamma0 + 78.0 + (elapsed - 120.0) * 0.15)

        stale = tnow - last_improve
        if stale < 10.0:
            target_gamma = max(gamma, target_gamma * 1.001)
        gamma = min(300.0, max(1.0, 0.9 * gamma + 0.1 * float(target_gamma)))

        if stale > 20.0:
            lr *= 0.7
            m1 *= 0.3
            m2 *= 0.3

        if stale > 90.0:
            if local_a is not None:
                y = _make_y_from_a(local_a)
            else:
                y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            y = y + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n)
            y -= y.mean()
            m1.fill(0.0)
            m2.fill(0.0)
            lr = float(lr0) * 0.7
            gamma = float(gamma0)
            last_improve = tnow

    if local_a is not None:
        sc = _safe_evaluate(local_a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = _scale_for_eval(local_a).copy()
            print(f"[{label}] final exact {best_score:.8f}", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"[{label}] done local_fast={local_fast:.8f}", flush=True)
    else:
        print(f"[{label}] done no-improvement", flush=True)

    return best_score, best_a


def optimize_linear(a0, n, deadline, best_score, best_a, label, exact_interval, max_exact, gamma0, lr0, rng_seed):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return best_score, best_a
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(rng_seed))
    a = _normalize_valid(a0, n)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    size = _pow2_at_least(3 * n - 2)
    m1 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    local_fast = np.inf
    local_a = None
    gamma = float(gamma0)
    lr = float(lr0)
    start_t = time.monotonic()
    last_improve = start_t
    last_exact = start_t
    exact_count = 0
    it = 0

    print(f"[{label}] start linear n={n} gamma0={gamma:.2f} lr0={lr:.4f}", flush=True)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0:
        it += 1
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
        cfull = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:2 * n - 1]
        c = np.maximum(cfull, 0.0)
        m = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            break
        fast = 2.0 * m / float(n)
        tnow = time.monotonic()

        if fast < local_fast - 1e-12:
            local_fast = fast
            local_a = a.copy()
            last_improve = tnow

        if local_a is not None and exact_count < max_exact and fast < float(best_score) - 1e-6 and (tnow - last_exact) > exact_interval and (deadline - tnow) > 30.0:
            sc = _safe_evaluate(local_a)
            exact_count += 1
            last_exact = tnow
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = _scale_for_eval(local_a).copy()
                print(f"[{label}] exact improved {sc:.8f} it={it}", flush=True)

        w = _make_weights(c, m, gamma)
        g = _grad_from_weights(a, w, fa, size)
        grad = 2.0 * (g - g.mean())

        m1 = 0.9 * m1 + 0.1 * grad
        m2 = 0.999 * m2 + 0.001 * (grad * grad)
        step = lr * m1 / (np.sqrt(m2) + 1e-6)
        mx_step = float(np.max(np.abs(step))) if step.size else 0.0
        if mx_step > 0.8:
            step = step * (0.8 / mx_step)

        an = a - step
        an = np.maximum(an, 0.0)
        s = float(an.sum())
        if s < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
            if local_a is not None:
                an = local_a.copy()
            else:
                an = a.copy()
            s = float(an.sum())
            if s < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
                an = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
                s = float(n)
        a = an / s * n

        if local_a is not None:
            sc = local_fast
            if sc < float(best_score) - 1e-4 and exact_count < max_exact and (tnow - last_exact) > exact_interval * 2.0 and (deadline - tnow) > 30.0:
                sc2 = _safe_evaluate(local_a)
                exact_count += 1
                last_exact = tnow
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_a = _scale_for_eval(local_a).copy()
                    print(f"[{label}] delayed exact improved {sc2:.8f} it={it}", flush=True)

        elapsed = tnow - start_t
        if elapsed < 30.0:
            target_gamma = gamma0 + elapsed * 0.2
        elif elapsed < 120.0:
            target_gamma = gamma0 + 6.0 + (elapsed - 30.0) * 0.8
        else:
            target_gamma = min(300.0, gamma0 + 78.0 + (elapsed - 120.0) * 0.15)

        stale = tnow - last_improve
        gamma = min(300.0, max(1.0, 0.9 * gamma + 0.1 * float(target_gamma)))

        if stale > 25.0:
            lr *= 0.7
            m1 *= 0.3
            m2 *= 0.3

        if stale > 100.0:
            if local_a is not None:
                base = local_a.copy()
            else:
                base = a.copy()
            mean = max(1.0, float(base.mean()))
            base = base + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03 * mean, n)
            base = np.maximum(base, 0.0)
            base = _normalize_valid(base, n)
            if base is None:
                base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            a = base
            m1.fill(0.0)
            m2.fill(0.0)
            lr = float(lr0) * 0.7
            gamma = float(gamma0)
            last_improve = tnow

    if local_a is not None:
        sc = _safe_evaluate(local_a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = _scale_for_eval(local_a).copy()
            print(f"[{label}] final exact {best_score:.8f}", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"[{label}] done local_fast={local_fast:.8f}", flush=True)
    else:
        print(f"[{label}] done no-improvement", flush=True)

    return best_score, best_a


def propose_candidate():
    # Minimize 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2 by:
    # - starting from previous best and many Fourier/power/endpoint seeds
    # - optimizing in log/simplex coordinates with weighted-logsum gradient
    # - periodic exact re-evaluation, restarts, and linear final polish
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + 2300.0

    raw_parent = _validate_sequence(globals().get("height_sequence_1", None), 2535)
    max_keep_n = 15000
    if raw_parent.size > max_keep_n:
        parent_arr = _resize_sequence(raw_parent, 8192)
    else:
        parent_arr = raw_parent
    parent_arr = _validate_sequence(parent_arr, 2535)
    if parent_arr.size == 0:
        parent_arr = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)

    initial_a = _scale_for_eval(parent_arr)
    if (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 30.0 and parent_arr.size <= 20000:
        sc = _safe_evaluate(initial_a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            best_score = sc
        else:
            best_score = _fast_score(parent_arr)
    else:
        best_score = _fast_score(parent_arr)

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = 2.0
    best_a = initial_a
    print(f"initial best score={best_score:.8f} n={best_a.size}", flush=True)

    parent_n = int(parent_arr.size)
    lengths = []
    if 50 <= parent_n <= 12000:
        lengths.append(parent_n)
    for cand in (4096, 8192):
        if cand not in lengths:
            lengths.append(cand)
    if not lengths:
        lengths = [4096, 8192]

    hp = [(8.0, 0.12), (20.0, 0.15), (5.0, 0.10), (40.0, 0.08), (15.0, 0.11)]
    seed_counter = 1000

    for li, n in enumerate(lengths):
        if deadline - time.monotonic() < 200.0:
            break
        n = int(n)
        if n < 50 or n > 12000:
            n = 4096

        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        if n <= 3000:
            budget = min(850.0, rem * 0.50)
        elif n <= 6000:
            budget = min(650.0, rem * 0.40)
        else:
            budget = min(520.0, rem * 0.35)
        if budget < 130.0:
            break

        if n <= 3500:
            exact_interval = 3.0
            max_exact = 60
        elif n <= 7000:
            exact_interval = 5.0
            max_exact = 25
        else:
            exact_interval = 8.0
            max_exact = 12

        is_parent = (n == parent_n)
        tgen = time.monotonic()

        if is_parent:
            if budget >= 160.0:
                ypar = _make_y_from_a(_resize_sequence(parent_arr, n))
                pdur = min(520.0, budget * 0.50)
                pdeadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + pdur)
                best_score, best_a = optimize_y(
                    ypar,
                    n,
                    pdeadline,
                    best_score,
                    best_a,
                    f"parent n={n}",
                    exact_interval,
                    max_exact,
                    8.0,
                    0.12,
                    710000 + seed_counter
                )

            seeds = _generate_seeds(n, None, seed_counter + 41 + li, top_k=3, max_fourier=180, max_endpoint=50, max_scored=210)
        else:
            seeds = _generate_seeds(n, parent_arr, seed_counter + 41 + li, top_k=3, max_fourier=220, max_endpoint=70, max_scored=260)
        seed_counter += 37

        consumed = time.monotonic() - tgen
        budget = max(0.0, budget - consumed)
        if budget < 60.0 or not seeds:
            continue

        durations = _allocate_durations(budget, len(seeds))
        for k, a in enumerate(seeds):
            if deadline - time.monotonic() < 40.0:
                break
            dur = float(durations[k]) if k < len(durations) else 0.0
            if dur < 30.0:
                break
            y = _make_y_from_a(a)
            gamma0, lr0 = hp[k % len(hp)]
            edeadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + dur - 2.0)
            if edeadline > deadline - 5.0:
                edeadline = deadline - 5.0
            best_score, best_a = optimize_y(
                y,
                n,
                edeadline,
                best_score,
                best_a,
                f"seed n={n} k={k}",
                exact_interval,
                max_exact,
                gamma0,
                lr0,
                900000 + seed_counter + 100 * li + k
            )
            seed_counter += 11

    remaining = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
    if best_a is not None and remaining > 80.0:
        cn = int(best_a.size)
        if cn < 50 or cn > 12000:
            cn = 4096
        a0 = _normalize_valid(best_a, cn)
        if a0 is None:
            a0 = np.ones(cn, dtype=np.float64)
        pol_dur = max(30.0, min(320.0, remaining - 40.0))
       พลdeadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + pol_dur)
        if cn <= 3500:
            pi, pm = 5.0, 18
        elif cn <= 7000:
            pi, pm = 8.0, 10
        else:
            pi, pm = 12.0, 6
        best_score, best_a = optimize_linear(
            a0,
            cn,
            พลdeadline,
            best_score,
            best_a,
            "polish",
            pi,
            pm,
            30.0,
            0.08,
            1234567
        )

    final_a = _scale_for_eval(best_a)
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 25.0:
        sc = _safe_evaluate(final_a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            best_score = sc
            final_a = _scale_for_eval(final_a)

    final_a = np.asarray(final_a, dtype=np.float64)
    final_a = np.nan_to_num(final_a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    final_a = np.clip(final_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if final_a.size == 0 or float(final_a.sum()) < 0.01:
        final_a = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
        final_a = _scale_for_eval(final_a)

    print(f"final reported score={best_score:.8f} n={final_a.size}", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in final_a]
```
